## B. Font architecture after the fix

```
public/fonts/                          committed, SIL OFL 1.1
  ├── Inter-latin-var.woff2     48KB
  ├── Fraunces-latin-var.woff2  67KB
  └── LICENSE-OFL.txt

lib/fonts.ts                           ← the ONLY next/font call site
  import localFont from "next/font/local"
  export const inter     → --font-inter
  export const fraunces  → --font-fraunces
  export const fontVariables = `${inter.variable} ${fraunces.variable}`
        │
        ├── app/layout.tsx      <html className={fontVariables}>
        └── app/not-found.tsx   <html className={fontVariables}>
```

`lib/fonts.ts` rather than exporting from `layout.tsx` because there are **two roots that render their own `<html>`** — the shop layout and the global not-found boundary, which renders outside every layout. That's the structural reason the duplicate existed. Next deduplicates module-scope `localFont` calls, so one copy of each face is emitted — confirmed: exactly two woff2 files in the clean build, where the previous build had nine (seven stale).

**A latent bug fixed in passing:** `not-found.tsx` applied only `inter.variable`, so `--font-fraunces` was undefined on the 404 and display headings there fell back to `ui-serif`/Georgia. Both variables now apply. This changes the 404's heading face from Georgia to Fraunces — I'm flagging it because you asked me not to modify the visual design; it's a correction toward the system rather than a design change, and easy to revert if you disagree.

## D→E. Old chrome → new chrome

| Removed | Replaced by |
|---|---|
| Two-row 99px navy header | One-row 56px paper `AppBar` |
| `SubNav` department strip | *(nothing — space reclaimed)* |
| `DepartmentDrawer` + `DrawerTrigger` + `navDrawer` store | `BrowsePopover` + `/browse` + mobile Browse tab |
| `SearchBar` (permanent inline field) | `SearchTrigger` + `SearchOverlay` |
| `LocaleMenu` (dead chevron control) | A locale line inside the account menu |
| `chrome/styles.ts` hover-box | Orphaned once the dark bar went |

## F. Desktop behaviour

`Kartly | Browse | Search products ⌘K | Help | Orders | Account | Cart`. Fixed with a constant spacer — not sticky — because a sticky element still occupies its original flow space, so compressing one shifts the whole page. Compression (56→48px, wordmark 0.9×) is driven by an `IntersectionObserver` sentinel at 200px toggling `data-scrolled` on `<html>`; **no scroll listener, no React state, no layout shift**.

## G. Mobile behaviour

52px bar (wordmark, search, cart only) + fixed 56px bottom tab bar with safe-area inset. Five real links, `aria-current="page"` on the active route, cart badge on the tab. The shell carries a matching spacer so the bar never covers content.

## H. Search overlay

Trigger is an `<a href="/s">` that only becomes an opener after mount — that's what keeps search working pre-hydration. Overlay wraps a real `<form action="/s" method="get">`. Full combobox pattern preserved (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `aria-activedescendant`, arrows/Home/End, Escape, focus restore). Suggestions still come from the existing `/api/suggest` — no second endpoint. Empty state shows recent searches (localStorage) and departments.

## I. `/browse`

Route shell, prerendered static, built from existing `getNavDepartments()` — real counts, real brands, nothing invented. Full discovery experience deferred to wave 2, as instructed.

## J. Test IDs

Six added (`searchTrigger`, `appBar`, `browseTrigger`, `accountArea`, `cartLink`, plus promoting `searchOverlay`/`bottomTabs`/`browsePopover` from reserved to live). Contract and mirror verified **in sync: 24 keys each, zero drift**.

## K–L. QA

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| `qa/navigation.mjs` **(new)** | **127/127** |
| `qa/smoke-final.mjs` | **22/22** (was 19) |
| `qa/responsive.mjs` | **231/231** (was 210 — `/browse` added) |

Two real defects found and fixed: the tab bar's `border-t` sat *outside* its 56px box, so the shell spacer was 1px short and the footer overlapped it; and my own assertion read `textContent` (which sees `display:none` text) instead of rendered `innerText`.

## M–Q. Validation

Typecheck **clean**. Build **clean**, `/browse` added, **120 PDPs still prerendered**. All routes 200, invalid PDP **404**. Search URL contract verified across `q`/`i`/`brand`/`sort`/`page`/`deals`/`rating`/`price` — all 200 with correct result counts. `/api/suggest` returns 7 suggestions. **0** third-party font requests, **0** unexpected 404s, **0** hydration warnings, **0** console/page errors (the single logged entry is the deliberate 404 navigation, confirmed by omitting that route). No horizontal overflow at any of the seven widths.

## R. Unrelated files touched — three, all reported as required

1. **`app/not-found.tsx`** — renders its own `<html>` and header outside every layout, so it still had a navy `bg-header` bar. That is global chrome and would have been the one navy surface left. Updated its bar and search button only; page content untouched.
2. **`components/cart/CartDrawer.tsx`** — its dark strip existed specifically to mirror the department drawer, which no longer exists. Restyled to follow the app bar. Behaviour untouched.

Wave 2 — Product Discovery

You are continuing the Kartly redesign after the completed Wave 1 checkpoint:

Commit:
f0d388b5d380a5f931b89ba539651d379f842e75
feat: redesign Kartly navigation chrome

Do ONLY Wave 2 in this turn.

OBJECTIVE

Redesign the product-discovery experience so Kartly has an original, grid-first shopping interface rather than an Amazon-derived product listing.

Wave 2 covers:

1. /browse
2. category/brand discovery
3. product listing/search result grid
4. product cards
5. filters
6. sorting
7. pagination
8. mobile filter/sort UX
9. discovery-related QA migration

Do NOT redesign the PDP, cart, checkout, orders, account, auth, or homepage content in this wave.

CORE DESIGN DIRECTION

Kartly should feel like its own commerce product.

Use the established Wave 0 design system:

- deep teal: #14594F
- teal hover: #0E453D
- teal tint: #E8F0EE
- paper: #FBFAF8
- surface: #FFFFFF
- sunk: #F3F1ED
- line: #E6E2DB
- line-strong: #CFC9BF
- ink: #1A1A18
- ink-2: #5C5951
- ink-3: #8B877E
- terracotta accent: #B4443A
- accent tint: #FBEEEC
- success: #1E7A4C

Typography:

- Fraunces for display/headline moments
- Inter for UI/body
- tabular numerals for prices, ratings, quantities

Use borders and spacing rather than Amazon-like heavy cards/shadows.

Do not introduce another visual language.

IMPORTANT DATA/BACKEND CONSTRAINT

The existing catalog is authoritative for this wave.

DO NOT:
- modify data/catalog.json
- regenerate catalog data
- modify image provenance
- modify public/products/
- invent products
- invent prices
- invent ratings
- invent review counts
- hardcode fake filter counts
- create mock API responses

The existing catalog/search infrastructure must remain behaviorally authoritative.

The catalog may still be used as the current source during this frontend redesign wave. Do NOT attempt the PostgreSQL/backend migration in Wave 2; that will be a separate controlled phase.

PROTECTED FILES

Do not modify unless absolutely required by an actual Wave 2 defect:

- data/catalog.json
- data/image-sources.json
- ATTRIBUTION.md
- scripts/fetch-images.mjs

- lib/catalog.ts

--------------------------------------------------
PART 1 — AUDIT EXISTING DISCOVERY
--------------------------------------------------

Before changing code, inspect:

- current /browse implementation
- existing search/listing route
- ProductCard
- discovery/filter components
- lib/search.ts
- lib/catalog.ts

- q

- brand

Do not silently rename or remove query parameters.

If an existing component can be redesigned cleanly, prefer modifying it over creating unnecessary parallel implementations.

--------------------------------------------------
PART 2 — REDESIGN /browse
--------------------------------------------------

Make /browse a genuine discovery landing page.

It should feel intentionally designed rather than like a placeholder.

Use the real catalog-derived departments/categories and counts.

Suggested structure:

- compact page intro
- "Browse all" / discovery heading
- department/category navigation
- category cards or compact category tiles
- useful brand/category discovery where the existing data supports it
- a product preview/discovery section using real products

Do not make /browse a giant dashboard.

Avoid:
- Amazon-style department mega grids
- generic SaaS dashboard cards
- fake statistics
- invented merchandising claims

The page should have strong visual hierarchy and whitespace.

Use the existing real category data.

--------------------------------------------------
PART 3 — PRODUCT CARD REDESIGN
--------------------------------------------------

Redesign ProductCard substantially.

The current Amazon-like card composition must NOT survive merely with new colors.

Create an original Kartly product-card structure.

Desired characteristics:

- product image as the dominant visual
- restrained metadata
- clear product title
- compact category/brand metadata where useful
- rating/review information
- strong but clean price treatment
- discount/deal treatment only when supported by real data
- clear add-to-cart interaction
- consistent card height/layout
- excellent mobile behavior

Prefer a composition similar to:

image
small metadata
product name
rating/reviews
price
action

but make the exact spacing, grouping, borders, and interaction distinctly Kartly.

Use existing image assets.

Do not create fake badges.

--------------------------------------------------
PART 4 — DISCOVERY LISTING
--------------------------------------------------

The experience should be grid-first.

Desktop concept:

- page heading/context
- result count/context
- filter controls
- sort control
- responsive product grid

Avoid reproducing the old Amazon-style left sidebar + dense results pattern.

-------------------------------------------

The exact design is yours, but it should remain consistent with Kartly's design system.

The grid should adapt cleanly across:

- desktop
- tablet
- mobile

--------------------------------------------------
PART 5 — FILTERS
--------------------------------------------------

Wave 2 complete. Nothing committed.

## 1. Files created (5)

`components/search/FacetBar.tsx` · `FacetGroup.tsx` · `FilterSheet.tsx` · `SortControl.tsx` · `scripts/qa/discovery.mjs`

## 2. Files modified (11)

`app/(shop)/s/page.tsx` (grid-first listing) · `app/(shop)/browse/page.tsx` (real discovery page) · `components/product/ProductCard.tsx` (rewritten) · `components/search/{ActiveFilters,Pagination}.tsx` (restyled, same link semantics) · `lib/testids.ts` · `scripts/qa/{selectors.mjs, COVERAGE.md}` · four call sites that just dropped `variant="grid"` (`CartView`, `ProductRail`, `OrdersView`, `RelatedProducts`) · capture log.

## 3. Files deleted (3)

`FilterRail.tsx` · `FilterShell.tsx` · `SortSelect.tsx` — each replaced, not duplicated.

## 4. Major UX changes

**Listing is grid-first and full width.** The 240px left rail is gone; facets are dropdowns above the grid. A 1440px viewport now shows 16 products where it showed 4 rows.

**One card composition, was three.** The full-width search row — the most Amazon-shaped thing in the app — is gone, along with an unused `mini` variant. The new card has a deliberate weight order: photograph → brand/title/price → rating/delivery. Rating collapses from five glyphs, a caret and a count to `4.2 (2,823)`. The discount chip moved onto the image.

**Filters:** desktop dropdowns + a slide-over for the full set; mobile gets the same sheet. `FacetGroup` renders both, so they cannot diverge. Sort became a menu of links instead of a `<select>` that navigated on every arrow key.

**`/browse` is a real destination**: departments with covers and counts, all brands, per-department brand entry points, and a reduced-now grid — all catalogue-derived.

## 5. URL behaviour preserved

Verified live: `q`, `i`, `brand`, `price`, `rating`, `deals`, `avail`, `sort`, `page`, plus the legacy `k=` alias — all 200 with correct counts. Nothing was moved into React state; every control is still a link built by the untouched `facetsToHref`.

## 6. QA results

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| `qa/discovery.mjs` **(new)** | **83/83** |
| `qa/navigation.mjs` | **127/127** |
| `qa/smoke-final.mjs` | **22/22** |
| `qa/responsive.mjs` | **231/231** |

Three real defects found and fixed during the wave:
- **`backdrop-filter` on the sticky bar made `position: fixed` children anchor to it**, collapsing the mobile filter sheet to a 120px strip. Fixed by portalling the sheet to `<body>`.
- **I rendered `FilterSheet` twice** (mobile + desktop variants), producing two dialogs and a duplicate `id` — the same duplicate-instance bug this project fixed once before. Now one instance positioned by flex order.
- **`/browse` brand links were 16px tall**, under the 24px minimum.

Two of my own assertions were wrong, not the app: an unscoped `a[href^='/s?i=']` matched the hidden search overlay's department list, and I asserted availability filtering must *narrow* the set.

## 7–8. Typecheck / build

Both clean. 120 PDPs still prerendered; `/browse` still static.

## 9. Responsive

231/231 across 375–1440. Grid steps 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 columns. No horizontal overflow anywhere.

## 10. Protected files

All twelve **unchanged** against `f0d388b`: catalog, image-sources, ATTRIBUTION, fetch-images, `public/products/` (120 files), commerce, search, catalog lib, suggest route, fonts, `.claude/`. No env/db/Prisma/secret files. `package.json` and lockfile unchanged — nothing installed. Test-ID contract in sync: 28 keys each side, zero drift.

## 11. `git status --short`

```
 M .agent-logs/2026-09-20_15-58-14_c4110ea9.md

?? components/search/FacetBar.tsx

```

`scripts/qa/COVERAGE.md` shows as modified in the diff but had already been staged-adjacent — it carries the Wave 2 migration table (old selector → new, kept/strengthened/obsolete per assertion).

## 12. Known remaining issues

1. **The page background is still the replica grey** (`--color-page: #eaeded`), so the paper-coloured control bar sits on grey with a visible seam. It's a one-line change but it repaints every page including the homepage, which is Wave 3's subject — the token is already annotated `wave 3`. Flagging because it's visible on Wave 2's own pages.
2. **The availability facet can never change the result set** — no product in the catalogue is out of stock. `lib/search.ts` decides whether to offer it and is protected, so I left it. Worth deciding in a later wave whether to hide a filter that cannot filter.
3. **Homepage still uses the old `ProductRail` and `CardRow` composition** around the new card — expected, Wave 3.
4. Legacy suites `qa-discovery`, `qa-p1`, `qa-home`, `visual-qa` still target the old listing and will fail; `qa-discovery` and the filter half of `qa-p1` are fully re-targeted by `qa/discovery.mjs` with the mapping documented. No suite deleted.

Wave 3 complete. Nothing committed.

## 1. Files created (4)

`components/home/Hero.tsx` · `CategoryMosaic.tsx` · `Shelf.tsx` · `scripts/qa/home.mjs`

## 2. Files modified (8)

`app/(shop)/page.tsx` (rewritten) · `app/globals.css` (page ground) · `components/home/RecentlyViewed.tsx` (Rail → Shelf) · `lib/testids.ts` · `scripts/qa/{selectors.mjs, COVERAGE.md}` · **`app/(auth)/layout.tsx`** and **`components/auth/SignInForm.tsx`** — background token only, explained below · capture log.

## 3. Files deleted (3)

`HeroCarousel.tsx` · `CardRow.tsx` · `ProductRail.tsx` — all three were homepage-only (audited: `ProductRail` had one other consumer, `RecentlyViewed`, which was migrated to `Shelf` first).

## 4. Homepage structure

Five sections, each answering a different question: **hero** (what is this shop) → **departments** (where do I start) → **best reviewed** (what's worth buying) → **reduced this week** (what's cheap now) → **Kartly's Choice + recently viewed** → **search CTA** (what if none of that suited). Ten modules became six.

## 5. Major design decisions

**The carousel is gone entirely.** Four rotating slides of invented copy, autoplaying every 6s, needing arrows, dots and a reduced-motion guard — three quarters of it written for nobody. Replaced by a static editorial hero whose claims are all checkable: product and department counts are counted, the delivery threshold comes from `COMMERCE`, and the three products shown are real and clickable.

**Asymmetric category mosaic** — two image-led feature panels plus a compact index, instead of ten identical tiles that gave the eye nowhere to land.

**One real correction mid-wave:** my first ranking picked the "two largest departments", but **every department holds exactly 12 products**, so the sort silently degenerated to alphabetical — and the code comment claimed otherwise. Switched to total review count (a real engagement signal, 63,423 → 27,733) and corrected both comments.

## 6. Page background

`body` now uses `--color-paper`; `--color-page: #eaeded` is **removed**, not orphaned. Audited all consumers first: checkout sets `bg-white` explicitly and is untouched (verified `rgb(255,255,255)` at runtime); auth was the only other explicit consumer, so its two `bg-page` references moved to `bg-paper` — otherwise `/signin` would have been the one grey surface left. Verified live: home/browse/search/help/signin all `rgb(251,250,248)`, checkout still white.

## 7. Product-card reuse — confirmed

**One card component on disk.** `Hero`, `CategoryMosaic`, `Shelf` and the reduced grid all render the canonical Wave 2 `ProductCard`. `Shelf` is a *container* refactor of `ProductRail` — its scroll mechanism was already correct and was kept; only the chrome changed. No second card variant exists.

## 8. URL behaviour preserved

Every homepage link resolves to an existing discovery URL, and each department link is *followed* in QA and asserted to return results. Wave 1 and Wave 2 behaviour intact: navigation 127/127, discovery 83/83, all filter/sort/page URLs still 200.

## 9. QA results

| Suite | Result |
|---|---|
| `qa/home.mjs` **(new)** | **60/60** |
| `qa/discovery.mjs` | **83/83** |
| `qa/navigation.mjs` | **127/127** |
| `qa/smoke-final.mjs` | **22/22** |
| `qa/responsive.mjs` | **231/231** |

`COVERAGE.md` carries the per-assertion `qa-home` mapping: carousel/slide/arrow/reduced-motion assertions marked **obsolete** (their subject no longer exists), "0 broken images" **kept verbatim**, department and card checks **kept and strengthened**. `qa-homepage-polish` marked obsolete. No legacy suite deleted or modified — 13 still on disk, 0 changed.

## 10–12. Typecheck / build / responsive

Typecheck clean. Build clean, **120 PDPs still prerendered**, `/browse` and `/` still static. Responsive 231/231 plus the homepage's own sweep at all seven required widths. Zero console, page or **hydration** errors; zero unexpected 404s; no horizontal overflow.

## 13. Protected files

All fourteen **unchanged** vs `4fc1d73`: catalog, image-sources, ATTRIBUTION, fetch-images, `public/products/`, commerce, search, catalog lib, suggest route, fonts, `.claude/`, **`package.json` and lockfile** (nothing installed). No Prisma, no `.env`, no database files. No invented products, prices, ratings, counts or discounts — QA asserts any struck price is a real number.

## 14. Known remaining issues

1. **Imagery remains the ceiling.** The hero's three products are the best-reviewed in Electronics/Sports/Pets, but the photographs are contextual stock, so the feature panels show a dog at a fountain and a cat rather than product shots. No layout change fixes that; it's a sourcing problem.
2. **`qa-home`, `qa-homepage-polish`, `visual-qa` still target the deleted carousel** and will fail. Documented as obsolete/superseded with mappings; suites preserved per instruction.
3. **`--color-header` and `--color-brand-deep` are now fully unused** (annotated "delete in wave 6") — harmless, just not yet removed.
4. **The `.card` utility is now used only by cart, checkout, help and 404** — storefront surfaces no longer use it. It'll retire as those waves land.

--------------------------------------------------
PART 1 — AUDIT THE EXISTING PDP
--------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------
PART 3 — PDP INFORMATION ARCHITECTURE
--------------------------------------------------

Create a strong Kartly PDP hierarchy.

A recommended conceptual structure is:

breadcrumb/context
        ↓
image gallery     product information
                  brand/category
                  title
                  rating/reviews
                  price
                  availability
                  variant selection
                  quantity
                  purchase actions
                  delivery information
        ↓
product details/specifications
        ↓
reviews
        ↓
related products

The exact implementation is yours.

The important requirement is clear hierarchy and strong whitespace.

Avoid copying Amazon's PDP structure.

Do NOT reproduce:

- Amazon-like dense right-side purchase box
- excessive bordered panels
- cluttered utility rows
- marketplace-style seller blocks
- fake urgency messaging
- excessive badges

Kartly should feel more editorial and intentional.

--------------------------------------------------
PART 4 — IMAGE GALLERY
--------------------------------------------------

Redesign the product image area.

If multiple product images exist:

PART 5 — PRODUCT INFORMATION
--------------------------------------------------

Design a clear product-information hierarchy:

- brand/category metadata
- product title
- rating/review summary
- price
- discount information only if actually present
- availability if actually present
- variant selection if actually present
- quantity
- primary purchase action

Use the existing PriceBlock, StarRating, QuantityStepper, Button, Badge and other established primitives where appropriate.

Do not create duplicate primitives unnecessarily.

Price should have strong visual hierarchy without becoming oversized.

--------------------------------------------------
PART 6 — VARIANTS
--------------------------------------------------

Inspect the actual variant model before redesigning it.

If variants exist:

- preserve the existing variant IDs
- preserve selected state

--------------------------------------------------
PART 7 — PURCHASE ACTIONS
--------------------------------------------------

- validate the product

--------------------------------------------------
PART 8 — DELIVERY / COMMERCE INFORMATION
--------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------

Treat mobile as a first-class PDP.

Wave 4 complete. Nothing committed.

## 1. Files created (1)

`scripts/qa/pdp.mjs`

## 2. Files modified (12)

`app/(shop)/dp/[id]/page.tsx` (rewritten) · `not-found.tsx` · `BuyBox.tsx` (rewritten) · `VariantPicker.tsx` (rewritten) · `Gallery.tsx` · `Reviews.tsx` (header/chrome) · `lib/testids.ts` · `qa/{selectors.mjs, COVERAGE.md}` · `app/(shop)/page.tsx` + `RecentlyViewed.tsx` (import path only, from the Shelf move) · capture log.

## 3. Files deleted / moved (2)

`RelatedProducts.tsx` **deleted** — it was a second container around the same card. `components/home/Shelf.tsx` → **`components/ui/Shelf.tsx`** (git-tracked rename), since it's now shared by homepage and PDP.

## 4. Information architecture

`breadcrumb → [gallery | decision column] → about (highlights + specification) → reviews → related → also viewed`

Three columns became two. The replica put the gallery left, spec text centre and a boxed purchase panel pinned right — three competing centres with the decision trapped in the narrowest column. Now the product is on the left and **the entire decision is one unbroken column** on the right: brand/category → title → rating → price → stock → variant → quantity → two actions → delivery and policy. Everything that is reference rather than decision moved below the fold where it gets full width.

## 5. Major design decisions

The purchase panel is **unboxed** — no bordered right-rail block, no lock icon, no seller table. Two clearly ranked actions (solid teal *Add to cart*, outline *Buy now*) replacing a yellow-and-orange pair of equal weight. Title set in Fraunces at 34px. The two generated "Product description" prose paragraphs were **removed**: they asserted a house style around real bullet text, and the bullets now stand on their own.

## 6. Gallery

Kept the existing derived-crop mechanism (the catalogue has one image per product; four framings are derived, verified distinct in an earlier wave). No carousel, no autoplay. Thumbnails are `role="tab"` with a **2px brand border** as selected state, 56–64px targets, keyboard-operable. On mobile they move **below** the main image via `flex-col-reverse`.

## 7. Variants

Model untouched — same IDs, same `PdpContext` state, same price delta. Selection is no longer signalled by an orange glow: swatches get a brand ring **plus a tick**, chips fill solid, and the current choice is written out in the legend. **Colour is never the only indicator.** A product with no variants renders no section. Verified: default variant's price delta is applied (the 2TB SSD defaults to its 1TB variant at −$60).

## 8. Purchase behaviour — preserved exactly

Same `useCart` store, same `openDrawer`, same `buyNow` routing to `/cart` rather than payment. Quantity still caps at `min(10, stock)`. **`lib/commerce.ts` untouched** — the delivery threshold is read from `COMMERCE`, not duplicated. One change: the quantity `<select>` became the app's canonical `QuantityStepper`, and the CTA label is now "Add to cart", matching the cards (the PDP previously said "Add to Cart").

## 9–10. Reviews / related

Review data, sorting, star filtering, histogram and helpful votes all unchanged — only the heading and section chrome moved to the new system. Related products use **`Shelf` + the canonical `ProductCard`**; there is exactly **one** ProductCard file and **no** PDP-only variant.

## 11. URL behaviour preserved

`generateStaticParams` intact — **120 PDPs still prerendered**. Valid product 200, invalid product still a real **404** with the designed page. Breadcrumbs link to `/browse` and `/s?i=<real category>`, both verified to resolve with results.

| `qa/pdp.mjs` **(new)** | **108/108** |
| `qa/home.mjs` | **60/60** |

**Three assertion errors were mine, corrected against real behaviour rather than weakened:** (1) I compared the rendered price to the bare catalogue price, ignoring that the default variant's delta applies; (2) I asserted a recently-viewed section on the PDP — there has never been one, the PDP records and the *homepage* displays, so the check now tests that real contract; (3) the smoke suite targeted `/^Add to Cart$/`, stale now that the casing inconsistency is gone — repointed at the test ID. All documented in COVERAGE.md.

## 13–15. Typecheck / build / responsive

Typecheck clean. Build clean, 120 PDPs prerendered, `/browse` and `/` still static, no route changes. Responsive 231/231 plus the PDP's own sweep at all seven widths — including a check that purchase controls never sit under the fixed tab bar. Zero console, page or **hydration** errors; zero unexpected 404s.

## 16. Protected files

All fourteen **unchanged** vs `0ef6f56`, including `lib/commerce.ts`, `lib/catalog.ts`, `lib/search.ts`, `package.json` and the lockfile. No Prisma, no `.env`, no database files, no secrets, nothing installed. No invented specifications, prices, ratings, review text or shipping claims — QA compares every rendered figure against `data/catalog.json` at runtime.

## 17. Known remaining issues

1. **`FrequentlyBoughtTogether` is still on the old visual system** — it renders on the PDP but I left its internals alone; its bundle logic is protected and `qa-fbt` is scheduled for Wave 5. It now looks slightly out of place between two redesigned sections.
2. **Gallery crops remain derived, not real alternate images.** Four framings of one photograph. Honest given the data, but a real second image would be better.
3. **Long titles dominate the decision column** — the headphones title runs seven lines at 34px. It's the real catalogue title; clamping it would hide product information.
4. **`.card` utility and `--color-line-soft` are now only used by cart/checkout/help** — they'll retire with those waves.
5. Legacy `qa-discovery`, `qa-p1`, `qa-purchase`, `qa-fbt` still reference the old PDP selectors and will fail; superseded portions are mapped in COVERAGE.md, and **no legacy suite was deleted**.
